Haan bhai, ab main is project ko **poori tarah samajh gaya**. Pehle ek zaroori baat, phir detail:

## ⚠️ Pehle ye jaan lo
Tumhara workspace `/workspace/project` **abhi khaali tha** — sirf `.git` folder tha, koi code nahi. Project ki files yahan nahi thin. Isliye maine tumhara repo GitHub se clone kiya aur andar ki zip extract ki. To haan — ab **saari files mere paas hain** aur maine padh li hain.

## 📦 Project kya hai — `NETO-DEV66/ALTERNATIVE-BOT`
- Repo mein sirf 2 cheezein hain: ek 1-line `README.md` aur **`ALTERNATIVE BOT.zip` (24.4 MB)**.
- Koi git history nahi (bas ek hi commit `a87702a` — "Add files via upload").
- Zip ke andar ek pura monorepo hai: **`rakazo-main/`** (≈3,773 files).

## 🤖 Rakazo kya hai
Ye **Rakazo** ka source code hai — ek **open-source, self-hosted AI agent platform** (upstream: `github.com/elie222/rakazo`, Apache-2.0 license). Ye "AI teammates" banata hai jo sirf chat tak seemit nahi, balki:

- **Persistent bots** — har bot ka apna conversation, memory, routines aur history hota hai (disposable chat nahi)
- **Computers** — bot browser, terminal, files aur graphical desktop use kar sakta hai (Team Computer shared, ya Private isolated)
- **Voice mode** — ElevenLabs / OpenAI / Cartesia / Fish Audio keys se bolna-sunna, calls
- **Multi-model (BYOK)** — apni LLM key laao, provider-neutral
- **Integrations** — Composio / Pipedream / MCP / OpenAPI tools
- **Sandbox providers** — Docker, E2B, Daytona, CreateOS, Boat
- Ye khud ko **Grok Bot, Meta Muse, OpenAI Dots, Claude Duet** etc. ka open-source alternative kehta hai

## 🏗️ Architecture (pnpm monorepo, TypeScript)
**Apps:**
- `apps/api` — backend (Hono + oRPC), business logic yahan
- `apps/web` — main web UI (React 19 + Vite + Tailwind)
- `apps/desktop` — Electron desktop app (web UI host karta hai)
- `apps/mobile` — Expo / React Native mobile app (iOS + Android)
- `apps/worker` — background jobs (Graphile Worker)
- `apps/www` — marketing website (Astro)

**Packages:**
- `core` — shared business logic, `contracts` — types/schemas, `adapters` + `adapter-kit` — provider abstraction (models/sandboxes/voice), `auth` — Better Auth, `db` — Prisma + PostgreSQL, `memory`, `chat-ui`, `ui-web`, `ui-tokens`, `logging`, `testkit`

**Baaki:** `infra/` (Docker Compose, Railway, systemd), `docs/` (51 docs), `scripts/`, `.github/workflows/` (CI, releases).

## 🛠️ Tech stack
TypeScript · React 19/Vite/Tailwind · Electron + Expo · Hono + oRPC · PostgreSQL + Prisma · Better Auth · Graphile Worker · Docker/E2B/Daytona/Boat.

## 🚀 Chalane ka tareeqa (README ke mutabiq)
Sirf Docker chahiye — `install-images.sh` script published images khींch kar web UI `http://127.0.0.1:5173` par chala deti hai. Ya full source: `pnpm install` + `docker compose up`. **Node 22.22.2 / 24 / 26 chahiye (23 aur 25 support nahi).**

Haan bhai, main project samajh gaya. Ye raha poora picture:

## Project kya hai
**Alternative Bot** — ek open-source, self-hosted AI agent/teammate product. Pehle ye "Rakazo" naam ka monorepo tha, ab rebrand ho raha hai. Iska core:
- **Persistent bots** — apni conversations, memory, routines, history
- **Voice mode**, **Team/Private computers** (browser, terminal, desktop access)
- **Bring-your-own model** credentials (Pi ke through), integrations (Composio/Pipedream)
- **Surfaces**: web, Electron desktop, Expo mobile (Android/iOS), aur Astro marketing site

## Repo layout (`/workspace/project`)
```
apps/     api, web, mobile, desktop, worker, www
packages/ adapter-kit, adapters, auth, chat-ui, contracts, core, db,
          logging, memory, testkit, ui-tokens, ui-web
infra/    sandboxes, systemd, compose
docs/     40+ guides (self-host, mobile-release, billing, etc.)
```
- Monorepo **pnpm + Turbo**, package scope ab `@alternativebot/*` (pehle `@rakazo/*`)
- **contracts** = shared zod schemas; **adapter-kit** = provider-neutral interfaces; **adapters** = vendor implementations; **db** = Prisma/Postgres
